# Full Jev catalog

[Reviewed picks](REVIEWED.md) · [Start here](../README.md) · [Search and filters](BROWSE.md) · [JSON](../data/catalog.json) · [Attribution](../SOURCES.md)

Evidence: **primary-source-reviewed** = public primary source inspected; **community-indexed** = attributed discovery, not independently reviewed here; **readme-matched** = explicit Jev and TypeSafe/System One references found automatically, not editorial approval. No entry implies a successful live test.

| Official TypeSafe skill | Official skill for writing Jev requests and choosing between Noul, Choice, and Score. | [repo](https://github.com/typesafe-ai/skills) · [demo](https://typesafe.ai) · [skill](https://github.com/typesafe-ai/skills/blob/main/skills/typesafe-ai/SKILL.md) · [evidence](https://github.com/typesafe-ai/skills/blob/main/skills/typesafe-ai/SKILL.md) | primary-source-reviewed / 2026-09-18 | MIT |
| Skillbox | Self-hosted skill library with optional Jev-based recommendations. Jev is an optional recommender, not required for the core library. | [repo](https://github.com/kitze/skillbox) · [skill](https://github.com/kitze/skillbox/blob/main/bootstrap/SKILL.md) · [evidence](https://github.com/kitze/skillbox) | primary-source-reviewed / 2026-09-18 | MIT |
| Tenbin | MCP server and skill for linting Jev questions, evaluating labeled examples, and choosing decision thresholds. Discovered through shipwithjev.com. Read the skill, SDK gateway, and ranking/batch-budget test source; no installation, upstream tests, or paid calls were run. Design and lint work offline; evaluation sends supplied state to TypeSafe. The host agent generates code, not Jev. Session limits use token estimates and usage accounting, not a guaranteed provider billing cap. Calibrate thresholds on separate labeled examples; listed performance and accuracy are not independently validated. | [repo](https://github.com/simota/tenbin) · [skill](https://github.com/simota/tenbin/blob/1ba0aa9646a559e6086d1488dedf9bd0a1f1c96e/skills/tenbin/SKILL.md) · [code](https://github.com/simota/tenbin/blob/1ba0aa9646a559e6086d1488dedf9bd0a1f1c96e/tenbin/src/client.ts) · [evidence](https://github.com/simota/tenbin/blob/1ba0aa9646a559e6086d1488dedf9bd0a1f1c96e/README.md) | primary-source-reviewed / 2026-09-23 | MIT |
| wellposed | Offline linter and agent skill for Jev requests: checks missing options, broken state references, and question types. Submitted in HN comment 49803528. Structural rules and tests were inspected, not executed. Structural lint is offline; --semantic makes paid TypeSafe calls and can send request state. Rules are heuristic and version-specific. The author's evaluation corpus is model-generated/labeled, so its reported scores are not independent validation. | [repo](https://github.com/suraj-phanindra/wellposed) · [skill](https://github.com/suraj-phanindra/wellposed/blob/main/skills/wellposed/SKILL.md) · [code](https://github.com/suraj-phanindra/wellposed/blob/main/skills/wellposed/scripts/structural.mjs) · [evidence](https://github.com/suraj-phanindra/wellposed/blob/main/README.md) | primary-source-reviewed / 2026-09-22 | MIT |

Choose the skill or plugin to avoid duplicate instructions. Speed claims are workflow-specific author measurements. | [repo](https://github.com/wy-coliney/jev-browser-use) · [evidence](https://github.com/wy-coliney/jev-browser-use/blob/main/README.md) | primary-source-reviewed / 2026-09-22 | MIT |
| Jev Cua (Codex/Waku) | Mac automation using Cua Driver, with optional Jev recommendations for saved workflows. Local runtime is not local Jev inference: the optional router sends best-effort-redacted requests and workflow descriptions to TypeSafe. Current README pins a specific Cua Driver version; review it before installing. Visual observations can disclose window contents to the connected client/model. Source-reviewed, not executed or security-audited. | [repo](https://github.com/Eronmmer/jev-cua) · [evidence](https://github.com/Eronmmer/jev-cua/blob/main/README.md) | primary-source-reviewed / 2026-09-22 | not-detected |
| Jev Social | Read-only Instagram, TikTok, and LinkedIn research loop: Jev Choice questions pick the platform and each bounded search, read, inspect, or finish operation; socai CLI executes in the user's Chrome and the app retains source-linked evidence. Maintainer submission. Requires OpenRouter Jev access, the socai CLI, and a logged-in Chrome session. Captured page, post, and comment text can enter Jev/OpenRouter requests; the app excludes raw DOM, tokens, cookies, headers, and local paths from public evidence. Actions are rebuilt from supported CLI capabilities and validated social URLs; TikTok media download appears only after explicit user intent. Source review covered routing, bounded action construction, target validation, evidence redaction, and project tests and documentation; this catalog did not run the application or paid inference. The recorded 64-second report is one dated local observation, not a benchmark. | [repo](https://github.com/socai-io/jev-social) · [demo](https://socai-io.github.io/jev-social/) · [skill](https://github.com/socai-io/jev-social/blob/46c0db23864b550dee8600b48a47d214eeaa251d/skills/jev-social/SKILL.md) · [code](https://github.com/socai-io/jev-social/blob/46c0db23864b550dee8600b48a47d214eeaa251d/src/actions.js) · [evidence](https://github.com/socai-io/jev-social/blob/46c0db23864b550dee8600b48a47d214eeaa251d/README.md) | primary-source-reviewed / 2026-09-23 | MIT |
